  - **Parent87 / Child67:** every hidden wrong concrete (6 of 6: skimmed cream and unpaid tips, shared expansion plans, disinheritance, notebook ideas, unapproved quotes, the double-promised promotion); `sheet_wrong_person` 0 hits on 6 sheets (fourth clean English purchase).
  - **`+fact` auto-apply tally since 2026-10-16 08:09 UTC (2 packages):** 2 applied (1 debatable on The Enthusiasts, 1 real here), 1 open real, none wrong. Not off.
  - **`+doc` / `+req2` / `+pub`:** 0 findings here; totals unchanged (1 judged `+doc`, 0 `+req2`, 0 `+pub` stored).
  - **`+sweep` (ADR-0168), first real run:** 0 findings against 10 real defects on this package (3 found by the main reviewer and `+fact`, 7 by hand), so recall 0 of 10 against the 80 percent screen, after 5 of 7 on the replay. Count of packages left in the window: 4 (`review_sweep_probe`). Too early to judge the sweeper, but the miss is the class it was built for (Nilla's twin of an already-fixed Rocky visibility claim).
- **Totals:** reviewer layers found 3 of 10 real defects (recall 30 percent on this package, 3 + 7 = 10), against the corpus 52 percent. No customer message sent.
- **Key files:** none in the repo; package content edited with exact-match SQL `replace` on 5 `mystery_characters` rows and `mystery_packages.detective_script`.

## Addendum 108 (2026-10-17): New-Purchase sweep - "The Great Pumpkin Heist At The D Loop Halloween Party" (EN chat, detective style, 9 characters, accomplice) - the whole Parent output came back in Portuguese

- **Alert:** `needs_review`, `accomplice_role_mismatch.has_accomplice=true,accomplice_role_count=2`. The auto-heal escalated ("master_context names no accomplice") because `master_context` was Portuguese and its lookup is English.
- **The real defect (new class, first in about 40 paid packages since 2026-10-01):** `master_context`, `game_overview`, `detective_script` ("ROTEIRO DO INVESTIGADOR"), `evidence_cards` and `materials` were all Portuguese on an all-English order. Input was clean: the chat has no Portuguese word, the webhook trigger logged `[Language] Detected "en"`, the nine character sheets (Child) came out English. The Parent prompt only says "match the conversation's language", so this is the Parent model drifting, not a pipeline input. Make module outputs are UI-only, so the module that first emitted Portuguese was not identified (open: look at the Make execution for scenario 9106101 if it recurs). Parent87 was the live Parent (imported 2026-10-14).
- **Why no gate saw it:** the materials-vs-overview check (Addendum 91) compares two Parent fields that were both Portuguese, and nothing compared the Parent output with the conversation. The package was only held by luck, by the unrelated duplicate-accomplice label (Priya).
- **Fix (decided, Jonathan said yes to about 0.10 USD):** translated only the four guest-facing fields into English with Sonnet 5.5 (8.8k tokens in, 7.7k out, about 0.14 USD), keeping the standard English headings, the trophy name ("Golden Pumpkin", the model had left "Abóbora Dourada") and the customer's "crock pot" (the model wrote "electric pot"); removed "dry ice" from the props list (preteens). `master_context` is internal and was left Portuguese (66k characters; the sheets already carry its facts). A full re-fire was not chosen: it would change facts the English sheets rely on and cost far more.
- **Detector and gate built the same day (free, deterministic, severe: the ADR-0131 test):** `package_parent_language_mismatch(_pkg)` / `list_packages_with_parent_language_mismatch(_since)` compare the stopword language of the conversation with the language of `game_overview` + `detective_script`; wired into `package_completion_blocking_defects()` as `parent_language_mismatch.*` (detection only, escalates to a human; the repair is a translation or a re-fire). Validated: 0 hits over every package since 2026-09-01 (no false positives), and it fires on a Portuguese clone of this package's overview. Migration `20261017020000_parent_language_mismatch_detector.sql`. Known limit: a customer who deliberately asks for another language than the chat would be held (the Golden Stars shape, ADR-0103 Addendum 99), which is a sweep decision, not a defect.
- **Sheet fixes by hand (free, all decided here):**
  - Priya Nambiar relabelled `accomplice` -> `suspect`: her sheet is an innocent sheet ("I did not take the Golden Pumpkin"), Harriet Oyelaran is the named accomplice in `master_context` and in her own confession. Priya's secret last sentence ("protecting Mrs. Oyelaran ... the full plan") reworded to plain loyalty so it no longer implies a plan.
  - Hidden facts that other sheets' questions or scripts announced: Toby's abandoned plan to hide the trophy ("you never told anyone") was stated as known in Marisol's, Harriet's and Imogen's Round 2 questions and in Josie's, Harriet's and Imogen's Round 2 scripts (and one pointform line each); rewritten to hunch questions ("did you ever think about...") like Wes's. Wes's score-adjustment request to Marisol (hidden) was stated by Harriet's and Imogen's Round 2 questions and Priya's script; removed. Wes's "secretly wrote down" points were known to six other sheets, so "secretly/privately" removed from his secret and background (smaller side matches the larger).
  - Two Round 4 questions were addressed to Mrs. Cravens (the host, not a player); redirected to Gwendolyn Fitch, who is at the island with her.
  - Gwendolyn's secret rested on an invented, unexplained "filling Josie must not eat" (not in the customer's concept); replaced with a concrete one (the cake is a consolation treat she means to cut after the final game) and "tampered with a child's testimony" softened to "leaned on". Josie's rumor that Gwendolyn "never hid" wanting someone else to win contradicted her "quietly hoping", so it now says she never cheers when Josie wins; Josie's question said "your son", now "your child" (no son exists in the cast).
- **Reviewer:** the gate held the package before the reviewer's first pass, then the reviewer ran AFTER my edits, so this package is NOT marked swept for recall (it would measure the residual). No `package_review_misses` rows. Reviewer findings and verdicts: main pass 3 (candy->cake and strap->handle, both from ambiguities in my translation, applied and real; "herself" in the pre-reveal Final Statements text, open and real, fixed by hand) plus a `+roster` finding that master_context spells Wes and Toby without their nicknames (false: master_context is internal, the sheets carry the nickname form; the roster heal renamed only Josie to "Josie Whitfield", cosmetic, left); `+p2` 1 (Wes's Round 2 question said Toby "drew on the scoreboard", which only Toby's own admission reveals: real, I had read past it, fixed); `+fact` 2 (Marisol's Round 4 script put Wes and Josie on opposite sides of her while three other sheets put Josie between Wes and Marisol: real, applied by the second pass, a duplicate stays open; Marisol's `round4_script_pointform` was resynced by the pipeline's `pointform_resync` right after, and reads "Place Josie beside you and Wes on her other side"; my own SQL update for it was declined and turned out not to be needed); `+leak` 0, `+doc` 0, `+pub` 0 (these ran after my edits, so the 0 for `+pub` does not measure recall on the Toby and Wes facts I had already removed). `+fact` tally since 2026-10-16 08:09 UTC: 3 applied (1 debatable, 2 real), none wrong. `+sweep` had not run when this was written.
- **Images:** three evidence images, no `image_judge` row at the time of the sweep; Round 2's clipboard has small pseudo-lettering on the blue band, Round 3 shows an ace of spades with purple glitter and black silk, Round 4 a gold-stained napkin. Left to the release pipeline's judge.
- **Measurements:** Child68 detective purchase counted at release (see CLAUDE.md item 2); `sheet_wrong_person` 0 hits on 9 sheets (fifth clean English purchase after Child67); no culprit-position pointer found in Round 1/2 fields of any innocent (0 against the baseline of 3); position contradictions about where another character stood: 2 in 9 sheets (Marisol's Round 4 side-by-side, found by `+fact`, and Nadia's "next to Priya and Toby" against the reading nook, found by hand), 0.22 per sheet against the Till Death 0.7. Child68 closed at 2 of 2 detective purchases.

### Update 1 (2026-10-17, same day): where the Portuguese came from, found from the stored data and the blueprint
- **Origin is one module.** `master_context` is two JSON objects from two Make modules. Object 1 (Detective Intrigue - Part 1 (Planning), module 2417: culprit, accomplice, relationship matrix, victim profile, crime method, conversation extraction) is entirely Portuguese; object 2 (Part 2 (Gameplay), module 4020: evidence, timeline, motives, host briefing) is English. Both carry the same instruction "Detect the language from the conversation and match it exactly", both received the same English conversation (6,910 characters, one clean webhook, `[Language] "en"`), so the drift is a single-module slip by the model, not an input or trigger problem. No Portuguese word exists anywhere in the Parent87 blueprint (checked), the title and cast names are English.
- **Why it cascaded:** the four guest-facing modules (Game Overview, Themed Materials, Detective Script, Evidence Cards) are told "Always respond in the same language as the master_context", and the materials module points at `victimProfile` / `methodDetails`, which live in Part 1. So one wrong module decided the language of all four. The Child sheets were English because the Child gets the trigger's `language` ("English") explicitly. The language of the Parent output is therefore decided by the model's own detection, never by the value the trigger already knows.
- **How often:** scanned every paid non-test package since 2026-08-11 (Sonnet 5 era) comparing the language of Part 1, Part 2 and the conversation: 1 real case (this one) plus Golden Stars (customer chose English over a Swedish chat, not a defect). About 1 in 100 or less. The new gate would not have been needed for Part 2 alone, and covers Part 1 through the overview/script.
- **Why the model slipped is not knowable:** Make module outputs are UI-only and the Make API token now answers 403 (scenario logs, teams). The prompt lists "If the conversation is in Portuguese, write in Portuguese" first in its example list, which is a plausible but unproven nudge.
- **Proposed prevention (not built, Jonathan decides):** every Parent module's `<language_instruction>` should say "Write ALL output in {{63.language}}" (the webhook already carries it; the Child blueprint already reads `{{63.language}}`) instead of asking the model to detect the language. That is a Parent change, so it should ship after the Parent88 measurement has 2 detective packages (CLAUDE.md item 1), bundled into Parent89 with the registry work if that is ever built, to avoid confounding the count. Until then the `parent_language_mismatch` gate holds a repeat before release.

### Update 2 (2026-10-17, same day): Parent89 built, folding the language pin in now
- **Decision (Jonathan: "should we not just fold this in and make parent 89?"):** build the pin now instead of waiting for the Parent88 measurement. The earlier reason to wait (a new Parent version would confound the count) does not hold: the pin does not touch what the measurement counts (hidden things whose content `master_context` never states), and Parent89 contains all of Parent88, so packages from the Parent88 import onward (parent_version 88 or 89) count together. Update 1's "ship after 2 Parent88 packages" is superseded.
- **What:** `temp-files/build-parent-v89.py` builds `MM Live - Parent89 (Language Pinned)` from Parent88. Only the `<language_instruction>` block of 24 Claude modules changes: Master Doc and Character Murder Parts 1+2 (171, 3000, 165, 4010), Detective and Character Intrigue Parts 1+2 (2417, 4020, 2453, 4030) and the 16 guest-facing text modules (Game Overview, Themed Materials, Detective/Investigator Script, Evidence Cards for R0 to R3) now say "write ALL output in {{63.language}}, the customer's language as already determined by the system", overriding other language hints in the prompt. The 4 Image Prompts modules are untouched. The script asserts: only those 24 mappers changed, each change reverses to Parent88 exactly, top-level fields unchanged, the source round-trips byte for byte. `{{63.language}}` is the webhook field `mystery-webhook-trigger` always sends (detected, `languageOverride`, profile, or "English") and the Child modules already read.
- **Trade-off:** the Parent used to self-detect the language from the chat, so a classifier mistake could not hurt it; now it trusts the same classifier the Child already trusts (Haiku on the first 3,000 characters, ADR-0112), so a misclassified chat would be wrong in Parent and Child alike, and the new gate would not catch it (it compares with the chat). Accepted: the classifier is the more reliable signal (1 slip in about 100 packages is the Parent's self-detection record) and a pinned `languageOverride` finally reaches the Parent (the Golden Stars case needed that).
- **Not imported.** Jonathan imports; on his word, log it in `prompt_version_history` (CLAUDE.md item 10 has the checks). Rollback: re-import Parent88.

### Update 3 (2026-10-17, close of session): `+sweep` result and Parent89 import
- **`+sweep` on this package:** ran at 15:32 UTC, after the reviewer's passes and all my hand edits: 0 findings, 0.39 USD. It found no residual defect after about 13 hand fixes, which says nothing about recall (the defects were gone before it read the package). Window count at 18:00 UTC: 1 (Pumpkin, Triwizard Tournament Murder and Dead Men's Cove used it).
- **Parent89 imported 18:30 UTC** (Jonathan: "89 imported"), logged in `prompt_version_history`. The two orders placed before the Parent88 import were Parent87; packages from 18:01 UTC on are 88 or 89 and count together for the hidden-thing measurement.

### Update 4 (2026-10-17, evening): the actual Part 1 input and output, pasted by Jonathan from the Make UI
- **Input (module 2417, Detective Intrigue Part 1):** clean. English title, English nine-character list, the English approved concept (6,910 characters), `claude-sonnet-5-5`, effort medium, adaptive thinking, temperature 1. The only Portuguese anywhere in the prompt is the example list in `<language_instruction>` ("If the conversation is in Portuguese, write in Portuguese. If in English... If in Spanish..."), where Portuguese comes first. So no substitution error, no leftover text, no missing conversation: the slip is the model's.
- **Output:** the JSON keys are English and every value is Brazilian Portuguese from the first field to the last (names kept, "Mrs." kept for the cast while the prose says "sra."; the English loanword "escorekeeper" appears inside Portuguese text). The Make Claude module returns only `jsonResponse` and `rawResponse`, so no thinking text is visible and why the model chose Portuguese cannot be learned from Make. Closed as unknowable; Parent89's `{{63.language}}` pin removes the example list that invites it.
- **New facts from the output that change two earlier calls:**
  - Gwendolyn's "filling Josie must not eat" was invented by Part 1 itself (relationship matrix, Gwendolyn and Cravens, `relevantSecrets`; the matrix also says her "son" never wins and then wishes "another family's daughter" would), not by the Child and not by the customer. The Child copied it; it was a Part 1 invention against the "no invented biographical facts" rule in the same prompt. My Addendum 108 rewrite of her secret stands.
  - The matrix names three cast members in short form ("Josie Whitfield", "Wes Okonkwo-Hart", "Toby Lindqvist") while the character list and the sheets use "Josephine 'Josie' Whitfield", "Wesley 'Wes' Okonkwo-Hart" and "Tobias 'Toby' Lindqvist". That is what the `+roster` finding reported: the roster heal renamed only Josie's sheet ("unresolved" for the other two). I had recorded that finding as `false`; it is `debatable` (a real mismatch in an internal document, cosmetic on the sheets, left as is on a delivered package). Verdict changed in `package_review_findings`.
- **Also noted, not changed:** the closing reminders of the Part 1 prompt still carry the murder-module sentence "VICTIM CONSISTENCY... same manner of death... the master_context", which contradicts the module's own "no one dies" rule and refers to a master_context that does not exist yet at Part 1. Probably harmless; fix it in the next Parent that touches that block.

## Addendum 109 (2026-10-17): New-Purchase sweep - "The Triwizard Tournament Murder: Scandal At The Champions' Tent" (`4b0f83c9`, EN chat, detective style, 6 players, no accomplice, Parent87 / Child68) - French evidence cards, `+fact` reversing a canon fact on 5 fields, first clean `+sweep` recall number

- **Package:** created 17:00 UTC, released 17:07, before the Parent88 import (18:01) and the Parent89 import (18:30), so it counts for neither. Every detector and `package_completion_blocking_defects()` came back clean; every defect below was found by reading or by a reviewer layer. Reviewer layers (main, `+p2`, `+fact`, `+leak`, `+doc`, `+pub`, `+sweep`) all ran BEFORE my first edit, so this is a valid recall measurement (`reviewer_ran_before_edit = true`).
- **Defects found and fixed (all free, all decided here):**
  1. **Evidence cards in French on an English order (C9, medium, class detect).** Rounds 3 and 4 fully French ("Rogue", "Drago"), the round 2 title French. The main reviewer translated the round 2 description to English; the `+p2` pass then translated one English sentence INTO French (wrong direction, verdict false). Rewritten in English in the standard format. Cause: the Parent language drift of Addendum 108 again (second sighting in two days); Parent89 (language pin) was imported later, so this package predates it.
  2. **`+fact` reversed a canon fact on 5 fields (C5, high for the package).** `master_context` has a seam: `victimLastSeen` says Viktor and Hermione last saw Rita alive near the refreshments table, `keyMomentPositions` (a later moment) has Viktor walking the maze edge alone. `+fact` took the later moment as canon and rewrote Hermione's introduction, relationships, R3 question and R4 script and Dumbledore's R3 question to put Viktor away from the table, while Viktor's own introduction, relationships, R3 question and R4 script still said Hermione stood beside him. All 5 applied edits are verdict false. Fix: Hermione and Dumbledore back to the original wording (the larger side), Hermione's two pointform lines resynced; Viktor's R3 script line (fixed by the main reviewer: "I did not touch the refreshments table after Rita took her drink") stays, it fits both moments.
  3. **Customer requirement lost (C20, REQUIREMENTS finding real):** the beetle was to hint at what Rita really was; no sheet or script carried it, and Hermione's secret was the abstract "the one fact that could destroy her career". Hermione's secret (`secret` and `secrets`) now says Rita is an unregistered Animagus who turns into a beetle; the Inspector's opening and Round 3 got one sentence each about the whispers (and that not every odd detail points to the killer, so the red herring survives).
  4. **"Two of you may have been placed far from this tent" (`+doc`, real):** four are (Dumbledore, Hermione, Snape, Draco). Now "Several of you".
  5. **Hidden fact announced by another sheet (C5, low, borderline):** McGonagall's R2 question to Draco said "Did she tell you she no longer needed you?", which is part of Draco's own secret. Now "Rumour says you fed Miss Skeeter gossip all year ... Was she still treating you as a friend lately?". Counted as 0 sightings for CLAUDE.md item 6, not as the Eddie class.
  6. **Round 4 image (C18, medium):** French pseudo-lettering ("La biore de surveriance") and no pocket watch; the image judge had passed it after its own re-rolls. One manual re-roll (standing yes, 0.04 USD, prompt with no mention of writing): a brass pocket watch with a cracked glass on blank parchment, no lettering, clean on the first try. Rounds 2 (blank aged page on a bound book) and 3 (stoppered vial, beetle) are clean as prop photos; round 3 does not show the crossed-out label, left as is because any prompt that mentions writing makes Flux draw pseudo-words (ADR-0165 lesson).
- **Left and recorded (decisions, not questions):** the "student" McGonagall protected is unnamed in the customer's concept and stays unnamed (naming one would invent facts about another character); the vial's origin in Snape's stores is canon in `keyMomentPositions` and Snape's secret but not spelled out in McGonagall's confession, which does not contradict it.
- **Reviewer verdicts:** `+fact` 5 applied, 5 false (one root cause). Main reviewer: "never reached her" real, round 2 French card real, Viktor R3 line real. `+p2`: "never spoke to her" real, Snape rumour grammar real, English-to-French card edit false. `+doc` 1 real. `+leak` 0 findings, `+pub` 0 findings, `+req2` none stored. REQUIREMENTS beetle real. **`+sweep` (ADR-0168): 7 findings, 7 real** (the 3 French cards, and 4 Hermione fields in the CORRECT direction, i.e. it saw what `+fact` got wrong). It missed the Dumbledore question, the beetle requirement, the abstract secret, the McGonagall question, the "Two of you" count and the image. Recall: 2 of 7 defect classes, about 7 of 13 fields (54 percent), against the 80 percent screen; precision 100 percent on 7. With Killer Sundae (0 of 10) that is 7 of 23 fields over two clean packages.
- **`+fact` switch:** the ADR-0165 rule says a wrong applied edit means set `review_apply_fact_findings` back to `off` and report. Done in the same SQL paste as the content fixes; the pass should not be re-enabled until it treats two canon fields that disagree as a conflict to escalate (never pick one and rewrite the rest), see ADR-0165 Update 2.
- **Detector gap and build (ADR-0131 test: cheap, deterministic, severe):** `package_parent_language_mismatch` reads only `game_overview` + `detective_script`, so French cards on an English package passed the gate. New `package_evidence_cards_language_mismatch(_pkg)` and `list_packages_with_evidence_cards_language_mismatch(_since)` (migration `20261017030000_evidence_cards_language_mismatch_detector.sql`), same stopword test on the cards. Corpus check from 2026-08-15 with the live function: 1 hit, this package (true); an ad hoc version on the first 6000 characters also hit "Murder Under Golden Stars" (Swedish chat, customer chose English, a decision), the live one does not. **Alert-only, NOT in the gate:** the repair is a free translation and a gate hit would hold a package like Golden Stars that is correct. Promote to the gate after about 10 packages with no false alarm. `materials` could be added to the same function later.
- **Measurements:** Child68, third detective purchase: culprit-position pointers in Round 1/2 fields 0 (against 3 on Till Death), `+leak` 0, position contradictions authored by Child68 about 1 to 2 in 6 sheets (Viktor's R3 "not near the table", Hermione's "never reached her"); the 5 `+fact` edits are not Child68's. `sheet_wrong_person` 0 hits on 6 sheets (sixth clean English purchase after Child67). Image judge: `r2=rerolled,r3=rerolled,r4=rerolled`, 0.19 USD, and it still passed the Round 4 image.
- **Key files:** `supabase/migrations/20261017030000_evidence_cards_language_mismatch_detector.sql`; package content edited by exact-match SQL. No customer message sent.
